ParlaMint-BG_2021-09-09.u4	Thank you, Mr. Chairman. Dear colleagues, I will read the Declaration of the parliamentary group of the party. There is such a people. A few days ago, it was 105 years old from the Good Age. The battles from 5 to 7 September 1916, the victorious drive of the Bulgarian army, the heroics of warriors from all parts of the country, the selfless support of Dobrudzhans made possible the release of South Dobrudja. We have no right to put these events behind us, we have no right to remain silent about the final return of South Dobrudja to Mother Bulgaria in 1940, which Dobrich will mark on 25 September this year. Conjunctual silence of the Bulgarian victory over the aggressors is disgraceful. For decades, young Bulgarians have grown up without historical memory of events that would be proud of the largest nations. Dobrudja, the cradle of our country, the land that remembers the enthusiasm of Asparuh's cavalry, deserves from this tribune to pay tribute to the heroes of the Good Epic. Bowed before the feat of General Ivan Kolev, General Todor Kantardzhiev, Major General Hristo Popov and the eternal glory of the hundreds of soldiers from the Bdin, Kozloduy and Primorski units of the Bulgarian army. In honor of this feat, we, the people's representatives in the 46th National Assembly, propose to support an old, patriotic initiative to return the old name of the 36th School of Sofia with this of the father of the Bulgarian Horseman General Ivan Kolev. And let this act be the beginning of our battle for national memory and a message to future generations, which must overcome our intrusive complex, that we are a small people whose freedom is donated, and democracy is an unending string of failures and contradictions. Knowing and remembering our history because the short-term peoples have a long transition. I will end with the words of General Kolev, who led in an uneven battle his troops: "Cavaliers, God is my witness that I am grateful to Russia for freeing us, but what are the Cossacks now looking for in our Dobrudzha? We will beat them and drive them away, like any enemy who hinders the unification of Bulgaria. Bow to the memory of the heroes of the Dobrudzha Epic! [[Loud applause.]]
ParlaMint-BG_2021-09-09.u5	Thank you, Mr. Hinoski. On behalf of a group, I give the floor to Mr. Atanasov.
ParlaMint-BG_2021-09-09.u6	Thank you, Madam President. Ladies and gentlemen, I take the floor on behalf of the Parliamentary Group of the Democratic Republic of Bulgaria to remind the Members of the National Assembly and the entire Bulgarian public that today is 9 September. [[Private applause from SG.]] On this date in 1944 a classic coup took place with the participation of a foreign army. The Bulgarian State is occupied by the Red Army and under the influence organized and logistically secured by this foreign army, a coup has taken place in Bulgaria, which subsequently allows the establishment of a communist regime, which 45 years separates the Bulgarian nation by different criteria. I want to, without sounding pathetic, but certain circumstances need to be recalled, because it is important to have a memory of what happened. Unfortunately, no reconciliation was found in Bulgaria for these 30 years after 1989. A few facts that are important because we are in the National Assembly. In 2000 the Commission decided to initiate the procedure laid down in Article 108 (3) of the Treaty on the Functioning of the European Union. The 38th National Assembly passed a law by which the communist regime was declared criminal. Although this law is declarative, it lists a number of crimes committed in respect of whole groups of the Bulgarian population people persecuted for their beliefs and for their origins. I just want to point out that formations of the so-called People's Court have been organized. Over 28 thousand people from the Bulgarian political, economic and intellectual elite have been arrested in Bulgaria for several months. 2730 death sentences were pronounced for comparison, the Nuremberg trial had only 12 death sentences; people were shot who fought for the Bulgarian national identity. I would like to point out that a well-known sub-chairman of the National Assembly Dimitar Peshev, who has organized the protection of the Bulgarian Jews, has been sentenced to 15 years in prison. Apart from that, people have been persecuted for these 45 years, they are confined to their rights, they are taken away from their development opportunities. The Bulgarian economy is ruined. Bulgaria from a prosperous country reaches a situation in which it went bankrupt in 1989. There are shameful things that have been done during these 45 years, such as the fact that the Bulgarian government during this period of time has proposed Bulgaria to lose its sovereignty and become the 16th Republic of the Soviet Union for this there are documents that are in history. So it is important to recall these facts, because there is a danger over the years that they will be flooded by the dust of history, and that is unacceptable. The Bulgarian Communist Party during these 45 years took action to divide the nation by a different sign, the last of which was the shameful revival process when the Bulgarian nation was divided by ethnicity. So in order for there to be reconciliation, there must be repentance. Thank you. [[Applause from DB.]]
